verbatim) after pausing brief 18. It's being built on `claude/peaceful-hamilton-59zdbb`, pushed and never force-pushed;
none of it is in `main`, and nothing is deployed.

**Completion, by the matrix:** 566 of 647 rows in scope Implemented And Verified (87.4%); 77 Implemented, Verification Incomplete, 0 Partially Implemented, 0 Not Implemented, 0 Blocked Externally, 4 Decision Required, 40 Explicitly Deferred.

  (Brief 19's work loop, on its builder's branch 2026-10-21: until it this said 566 of 640. Its seven commands are rows of
  their own, Implemented, Verification Incomplete until an audit row reads them.)

  (Brief 19's core, merged 2026-10-21: until it this said 566 of 636. Its four commands are rows of their own,
  Implemented, Verification Incomplete until an audit row reads them, and 46 measured rows read the engine's whole
  source, so the understudy's, the clock's and the core's changes leave them measured before what they read last
  changed, until brief 19's final runs measure them again.)

- **Read first:** `docs/verification/career-2026-10-20/criteria.md` (the items, each Open until the game does it and a
  test fails without it), `design.md` (binding: the agent's limits, the pathways, the record, the agreement, the work
  loop, who builds what) and the research in `docs/research/career-2026-10-20/`.
- **Merged so far, each from a builder's branch with its report:** the understudy's part (D6, brief 18's U1,
  `understudy/REPORT.md`); the clock (D1 to D5, brief 18's S1 to S5, `time/REPORT.md`); the core, the professional
  record, the agent's data, the work's data and the portfolio (`core/REPORT.md`). Brief 18's criteria mark its five
  items done here.
- **Still to build:** the showcase (W1 to W3); the agent's relationship, interest and agreement (G2 to G8); the work
  loop, its pay and commission, work eligibility and the tutorial's job guard (J1 to J6, D7 to D9, A3); then the
  playthroughs V1 to V18, the built app at both widths, and the gate.
- **An owner decision this brief leaves open:** the agent is a real person, depicted only from what's published, and
  marked fictional wherever he speaks. Written permission, or a fictional agent in his place (one data entry), is the
  owner's call.

## 3. What Brief 17 Did

The record is `docs/verification/continuity-2026-10-20/`:
- `README.md` has what changed, the measures before and after with how each was counted, and the gameplay comparison;
- `PLAYTEST.md` is the owner's route;
- `docs/text/SURFACES.md` is the coverage matrix.

- **The coverage matrix** (`docs/text/SURFACES.md`, `docs/text/surfaces/*.json`): 265 surfaces a player can reach,
  claiming all 4,230 sources of player text. Each row has its states, what reading it in context found, what changed or
  why it was kept, and evidence that opens from the repository. `tools/surfaces.mjs --check` is now the gate's
  twenty-first check.
- **The feedback** (`src/engine/systems/feedback.ts`):
  - five course voices, kept apart by a test;
  - the rule said as a rule ("A piece shown with it at 75% or more puts the note right."), never "taken";
  - each situation the brief names, and a success acknowledged and left there;
  - no "Meanwhile, try this";
  - a memory of everything the student was told, from the save's own feedback, with no randomness drawn and nothing
    added to the save.
- **Directors** speak in three voices, one a production. **Teachers** write their own term notes. **Classmates** each
  ask in a way of their own, and remember the last answer rightly.
- **The incident fix, the one authorized change to play:** a classmate's apology covers the misses since their last
  and marks each (`missesOwed`, `PartnerRehearsal.apologized`), so it never comes again for them. A new miss gets a new
  apology, and a late one says how late. A save from before is marked once on opening (`school.missesMarked`). The
  schema stays 94.
- **Eight editors**, each in a clone, read their surfaces where a player meets them and put what matters, what to do,
  the cost and the deadline before the button that commits it: material, school, money, health, profile, frame,
  papers, and the tutorial and help. The tutorial editor followed all 17 chapters and 70 entries as a new player at
  1440 and 390.
- **What the final runs found, put right before the tested commit:**
  - whole messages said again and openings repeated within a course's last five, both from the memory's reach;
  - eight sentences opening on "Nothing";
  - "put right on February 23" said on February 23;
  - six gate checks behind, found by running the full gate, which hadn't run since brief 16's merge.
- **What reading the final runs found in the tools, put right after the tested commit,** each with its lanes played
  again (`runs/README.md`, Found In The Runs):
  - the journeys' check 8 failed F1 for a warning that counted a piece revised to Working after it;
  - the advice harness's S3 read 1,836 standings as stale for naming the aim that took over;
  - the journeys' recheck wrote each journey's first three parts again without their counts.
  None was the game's.

## 3. What Brief 13 Did

Three areas, each in its own clone, worked from instructions written before their work
(`docs/verification/playthroughs-2026-10-17/prompts/`). The coordinator integrated them and ran the final verification.

- **Section 1, ST6-R2.c: verified** (`acting-criteria.md`, committed in `e1ed27fa` before any test, tool or measurement;
  `acting-result.json`; `acting/trace.json`).
  - **The ten readings at ±0.01:** one cause, `grainOf`'s internal quantization. It keeps the grain's share of
    difficulty to a hundredth, and at ±0.01 every share is under half of one (0.0005 to 0.0039), so the grain comes to
    nothing. That isn't display rounding, which is the result's tenth, and it isn't a gameplay limit, since no floor,
    hold or cap is reached. Carried at full precision, each would move the right way, by 0.063 at most. From ±0.02 every
    take moves the right way, and none of 784 moves the wrong way. The hundredth had been undocumented; `grainOf`'s
    comment now says it.
  - **"Unrelated," from the engine's dependency path, not a cutoff:** each ability is direct, small, indirect or none,
    on each piece, from the context the judge reads. 1/12 wasn't adopted.
  - **The 65 of 6,344 pairs:** none involves an unrelated ability. Each lead is the equal budget's arithmetic, against
    the grain and under a tenth, where believability alone is the bottom of the even actor's take.
  - **The rows:** c21 to c43 are met for fourteen actors, and nine tests were added to `tests/decisions-judge.test.ts`.
    The mutations h1 to h14 were all killed.
  - **History:** brief 12's (c2) and (c4) stay not met as written, and nothing was passed retroactively.
- **Section 2, ST6-R2.e: still the owner's** (Decision Required). (e1) holds. The ceiling cases are kept, the
  comparison actors were made below the ceiling before measuring, the material is the catalog's, and the fresh actors
  were run once. (e2) and (e3) aren't met as written. On some pieces the scene's grain, not the abilities, decides the
  margin, and on stage size's ten gains fall too heavily on one play for (e3.5)'s floor. The area's question and recommendation are in section 4.
- **Section 4, the journeys** (`journeys-criteria.md`, its expectations written before any ran; `journeys-result.json`):
  - **25 runs:** 23 complete school journeys and 2 focused scenarios, on 19 seeds (each advice triple shares one), from
    creation to the end, each with ten checks. 22 graduated, two of them in 2030 after a repeated year or a medical
    leave. The two who neglected the coursework (J15, J16) were dismissed, as expected.
  - **J21 didn't end as expected:** SLATE's mortality draw (`systems/mortality.ts`) ended the life at 26, on 9 November
    2028. The area recorded it as not a defect and added J23 to the same health profile; J23 graduated. Whether a draw
    like that belongs in a school journey is the owner's to judge.
  - **The players:** beginners, generalists, specialists and mastered actors, who follow, partly follow or ignore the
    advice, with missed work, money held back and illness or injury. Some fail by neglect, as expected, and some
    recover.
  - **Five game defects fixed,** each with a test:
    - D1, a classmate's evening after the School's end;

## 2. What Brief 10 Did, And Where It's Written

`docs/verification/decisions-2026-10-16/brief-10.md` is the account: every decision's status, each criterion before and
after, the regression, the final verification and the owner's decisions. In short:

- **Decision 1, the acting criteria:** ST3-R, ST6-R, H4-R and L3-R written and justified before any tuning; the originals
  kept and superseded. ST3-R, H4-R (12 of 12), L3-R and M1 met; **ST6-R not met on (a), (c), (e), (g)**, its (b′)
  proposed. Two public meetings for presence; the restrained specialist's budget made equal (it was 645.6 against

  owner to paste it, and don't invent what it leaves out.

## 4. The Game's Scope

- **In it:**
  - creation;
  - a Yale School of Drama student's life in New Haven: classes, coursework, productions and casting, the term's review,
    the degree;
  - the body: sleep, looking after yourself, health and cover;
  - banking: five US banks, and a home-country account kept from before Yale where its bank's terms allow;
  - papers: US and Connecticut tax, documents, immigration status;
  - practice briefs, 149 genres, the attributes and ways of playing.
- **Removed by the owner's scope reset of 2026-10-09, and to stay removed** (`docs/REQUIREMENTS.md`, "Retired by the
  owner's scope reset"; `docs/UNBITTEN.md`, "The scope reset"):
  - the home and its rent and bills, and the rented room;
  - travel and stays, and cars and every other vehicle;
  - land and building;
  - the career: the industry, the agencies, and the school's way into it;
  - shops and possessions, the wardrobe, the kitchen, pets and the catalogs;
  - the assistant, the social life and the rail.

  The banking decision of 2026-10-16 restored no travel or relocation, and no parental leave was added. Nothing restores
  any of it without the owner.

## 5. The Rules, The Schema And The Model

- **`RULESET`** is `evaluation-8 (2026-10-16)` (`content/tasks.ts`). A result kept under another ruleset is shown as it
  was, not judged again. What each of evaluation-4 to 8 changed: `docs/ENGINE-MODEL.md` §10.
- **The save schema** is 94, in `SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION` (`src/engine/domain/model.ts`) and `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION`
  (`base44/shared/guard.js`). Move both together, by hand (CLAUDE.md, rule 14).
- **The formulas a take is judged by,** each in its file, every size SLATE's own design unless its account cites a
  source:
  - **Preparation:** each part is read against what completes it, and the least counts. A part's factor is floor plus
    (1 − floor) times min(1, prepared over required). The floor is `EVALUATION.prepFloor`, 0.35, and 0.75 for knowing the
    genre (`partFloor`) (`systems/evaluation.ts`).
  - **Suitability** (`systems/interpretation.ts`, `content/interpretation.ts`): the sum of a scene's signals, halved and
    held to −1 to 1, rounded to 0.01. The signals:
    - the register's concerns and the reader, each at half (`GRAIN.concern`, `GRAIN.reader`);
    - the medium: the camera a quarter for restraint, and the stage and the microphone nothing;
    - its tones, by share;
    - its turns, up to three, 0.15 each;
    - its features, by level; the voice isn't counted on stage;
    - its circumstances, a quarter a level;
    - its language, and the reading taken.

    A side Suits The Scene at 0.25 or more and goes Against The Grain at −0.25 or less (`GRAIN.band`).
  - **What suitability moves:**
    - **Difficulty.** On each dimension a side leans on, it moves 0.3 × s × the stakes' scale × (lean − 1) ÷ (its most
      − 1), never below `GRAIN.floor`'s bound (`grainOf`).
    - **The choice's rehearsal mark.** 0.9 − 0.1 × mean s, held to 0.8 to 1.0 (`requiredFor`). A step of rehearsal
      rehearses the choice in by 0.9 of its gain, of what's left, until that mark (`choiceMark` for coursework).
  - **The stage:** a house weighs execution 1.2 (`STAGE_LEAN`, `content/approaches.ts`).
  - **Mastery:** at full capacity difficulty asks nothing, so every side that fits reaches 100. Suitability is never a cap.
  - **The term:** the review counts a piece shown again at its second band, and one missed unexcused as due and Unready.
    It reads the term at 3 or more due, with 50% Unready short of the mark (`ESTIMATE.coursework`). The warning comes
    once a term, at 6 due and 35% (`TERM_CHECK`, `termTally`).
- **Criteria, each written before its measurement:**
  - interpretation I1 to I5 (`docs/verification/interpretation-2026-10-16/criteria.md`);
  - the genre signature S1 to S4 (`docs/verification/genres-2026-10-16/g1-replacement.md`);
  - the stage ST1 to ST8, F1 and F2 (`docs/verification/stage-2026-10-16/criteria.md`);
  - the attributes' criteria (`docs/verification/attributes-genres-2026-10-16/acceptance-criteria.md`);
  - the advice's (`docs/verification/advice-2026-10-16/criteria.md`).
- **Research:** the genres' (`src/engine/content/genre-demands.json`, from `docs/research/attributes/`); the stage's
  (`docs/verification/stage-2026-10-16/research.md`); 60 banks' terms (`docs/research/banking/home-accounts.json`).
  Most of the genre research is at low confidence, and a register's basis is shown as research or SLATE's reading.

## 6. Known Issues, And How To Reproduce Them

- **ST3, ST4, ST6:** `npx vite-node tools/interpretation.ts -- --fresh --stage --out <file>`, read against the stage
  account's table. `tools/genre-audit.ts` lists each genre's stage scenes by the side they suit.
- **H4 and L3:**
  - the criteria: `npx vite-node tools/ag-baseline.ts -- --out <file>`;
  - L3: `npx vite-node tools/brief-journeys.ts -- --profile specialist --seed bj-0 --days 120 --write <file>`.

  Both are as `phase-2b.md` ran them.
- **A term that misses pieces:** no test plays one. `tests/workload.test.ts`, "found by the sweep", sets the misses by
  hand and calls `termWarning`.
- **The gate's test workers:** `VITEST_MAX_FORKS` set below the CPU count, alone, stops vitest before any test ("minThreads
  and maxThreads must not conflict", seen here with 2 on 4 CPUs). Set `VITEST_MIN_FORKS` with it, or neither.
- **A shallow clone** makes merged branches look unmerged: `git fetch --unshallow origin` before comparing branches.
- **The built app's two skipped steps** have been skipped since 2026-10-12, because no scenario save reaches them: Work
  It Through's way kept or put back, and the rehearsal controls of a part with calls.

After pull request #32 the owner asked: "do a sweep throughout the entire code/game and make sure everything is up to
the standard I want it to be and then push and merge all information and all notes so I can pick this up in an
entirely new session." Done on `claude/zen-mccarthy-i88ujw` from `main` at `0d66ce60`, in pull request #33, merged to
`main` at that word. Not deployed.

## Where It Stands

- **`main` carries everything.** Every brief of 2026-10-16 was merged: #28 to #30 (`claude/remaining-acceptance`, then
  this branch: banking's home accounts, the attributes' phases 1 to 2b, banking's defects and skeptics), #31 (genre
  differentiation, interpretation, advice, the school workload), #32 (S3 and the stage) and #33 (this sweep). Nothing
  is on a branch alone.

- **The rules:** `RULESET` is `evaluation-8 (2026-10-16)`; the save schema is 94 in both places (`SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION`,
  `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION`). The sweep moved neither.
- **The checklist** is `docs/verification/WORKPLAN-2026-10-16.md`, sections 1 to 9. Read its Resume Here first.

## What The Sweep Found And Did

Three audits ran, of the docs, of the source's comments, and of the week's code for bugs. Each finding was read against
the code before anything changed, and each figure here was measured again, not taken from an audit. Their reach:
- the docs: the handoff, the work plan, the accounts, the top-level docs;
- the comments: every claim in `src/`, `tools/` and `tests/` that the week's changes could have falsified;
- the bug review: the week's code, `5b27379a..0d66ce60` in `src/` and `tools/`.
Older code was held by the gate and the reviews before it, not read again line by line.

- **In the game**, each fix with a test that fails with the old rule put back:
  - **A choice's rehearsal stalled short of its mark.** Once rehearsal's own part was full, a step stopped rehearsing a
    choice in at 90%, the mark from before suitability moved it to 80-100%. A side against the grain stalled near
    93.5%: a mastered actor prepared twenty days taped 99.3, not 100, on brief-rockumentary-2 played Set, which asks 97%.
    Briefs and coursework now read the scene's own mark (`requiredFor`, `choiceMark`). Of the sides that fit, 16 of
    1,769 on briefs and 49 of 511 on class scenes and plays ask more than 93.5%. (The commit's message says "49
    class-scene sides"; the plays are among them.)
  - **The term's warning counted otherwise than the review it warns of.** A piece missed unexcused is due and Unready to
    the review and was nothing to the warning: six shown with two Unready, then two missed, was 2 of 6 to the warning
    and 4 of 8 to the review. Both read `termTally`, and the
    warning is looked at after every piece due, missed or shown again.
  - **Its words.** The part most often short was grouped by its key across templates, and on a played term said 19
    pieces had a part by a name and remedy that 4 had (`mostShort`). A scene's table work was answered by "its own
    step" (now the Table Work's questions). "Still to come" counted what was set; "a notice of concern follows" was
    true only from good standing (now: the standing falls a step).
  - **Shown Today and Due Soonest** banded a smaller piece by its score, where the showing caps it at Working (`bandFor`).
  - **A scene's circumstances** had a reason from the text for each, written for all 86 class scenes and plays, and no
    screen showed one. What It Asks lists them now, as it does a feature.
- **In the tools.** `tools/workload.ts` reads each piece's short parts at its showing, as the showing does. On London's
  lives-0 the third term's partner part read 14 short at the run's end and 10 at the showing. `tools/scenario-saves.mjs`
  writes the acceptance run's 14 saves; until now that recipe lived in a session's scratchpad.
- **In the notes.**
  - Ten comments and screen notes the stage change had falsified, and a comment citing a test that didn't exist.
  - The guide's "complete at 90%" in coursework, briefs and productions.
  - Every account's "not merged".
  - The gate's seventeen checks (AGENTS.md said 15, README.md twelve).
  - Saves in IndexedDB, in AGENTS.md.
  - ENGINE-MODEL.md, written for evaluation-3, now says so and indexes evaluation-4 to -8.
  - The genre coverage page now lists the register.
  - The stale lines of the sections below are marked superseded in place.
- **Not changed:** no evaluation formula, threshold, ruleset or schema.

# Earlier: The Genre Benchmark S3, Resolved, 2026-10-16

History now: the section above is the current status. It covers the owner's brief "Resolve The
Remaining Genre Benchmark With Defensible Changes And Proof", on `claude/zen-mccarthy-i88ujw`, restarted from `main` at
`eb0b6022` after pull request #31 was merged. A new pull request is opened for the owner: not merged, not deployed.
**Superseded:** that was #32, merged as `0d66ce60`.
The account is `docs/verification/genres-2026-10-16/g1-replacement.md` ("S3: The Reader Weighed As A Concern"), and
its evidence is in `s3-reader/`.

- **The diagnosis**, on the 78.9% sample (22,432 of 28,413):
  - The reader alone decided 40% of the 5,981 misses.
  - The register's half weight against every scene signal decided 62%.
  - The scene's own signals decided 26% whatever was changed.
  - The cap and ties decided under 4%.
  - No miss was a mislabeled pair.
- **The change.** `GRAIN.reader` is 0.5, from 1: the reader counts as a register concern does (`GRAIN.concern`), where
  before it counted twice as much, and nothing recorded why. `RULESET` is evaluation-7. Nothing else moved: not the
  threshold, the samples, the pick or the genre data.
- **The results**, from the tool's numerators:
  - fresh held-out: 22,432 to 23,300 of 28,413 (78.95% to 82.00%);
  - held-out: 81.00% to 82.84%;
  - fresh designed: 86.71% to 87.40%;
  - designed: 88.98% to 88.90%, 12 cases fewer.
- **Fresh validation, defined before it ran** (`fade7e1c`): second readings, contrasting pairs off S3's grid, and
  two-ability profiles.
  - 1,015,539 of 1,156,538 differ (87.8%), against 86.3% before. It passed at both weights, so it shows the change
    generalizes but doesn't discriminate it.
  - Related pairs differ in 57.2%.
  - With the genre ignored, 0 differ.
- **What's kept.**

## What the pass did

1. **The guide's reference** (`src/engine/content/guide/`): 68 entries, one press away from every feature (How This
   Works), with search, contents by goal, typed Connected Systems, a breadcrumb trail, Show Me Where and stable links.
   - It runs nothing and holds the clock.
   - Every worked example is played as a test on a throwaway save (`tests/guide-examples-*.test.ts`).
2. **A piece judged in its segments** (`systems/piece.ts`): beats, tones and movements, with stamina as a drain, slips
   and recovery, and continuity shown.
3. **The scene together, kept apart from your own** (`systems/ensemble.ts`): a mastered player's own result is 100
   whatever the partner does.
4. **Rehearsal with memory** (`systems/workbook.ts`; `work.identify`, `work.try`, `work.settle`): problems, ways,
   evidence, kept or put back. A kept way is rehearsed in by later work on the feet.
5. **Direction that can be answered** (`systems/production.ts`): craft notes with ways, an addressed record, and later
   notes answering it.
6. **The actor the work so far shows** (`systems/artistry.ts`): it informs suggestions, recommendations and craft notes,
   and changes no result.
7. **Two briefs for every genre:** 298 briefs with 648 readings, with sizes, stakes and how the reader plays.
8. **The specialist question settled** against a stated criterion, with the remaining trade-off explained, not hidden.
9. **A defect pass in three rounds plus a prose pass** (§17 of the report): every fix reproduced, tested and
   mutation-checked.
10. **The registries held to one standard:** every player command is in a capability row, by check.

# Earlier: Attributes, Genres And Their Integration, 2026-10-12

The owner's brief was "Bring SLATE’s Attributes, Genres, And Their Integration To The Highest Demonstrable Standard",
from `main` at `e53eaa89`. The evidence, requirement by requirement, is
`docs/verification/ATTRIBUTES-GENRES-PASS-2026-10-12.md`; the model is `docs/ENGINE-MODEL.md` §8.

- **Branch:** `claude/attributes-genres`, pushed.
- **Merged:** at the owner's "Merge it.", `main` was fast-forwarded from `e53eaa89` to this pass.
- **Not deployed.**
- **Saves:** schema 94 is unchanged.
  - `person.briefs` is a new optional field, written on first use.
  - A save from before keeps every result it has.
  - Its next self-tape asks for a reading first.
- **Untouched:**
  - the stash (`stash@{0}`);
  - the three worktrees;
  - the untracked files at the root.
- **Kept deferred:**
  - the 16 deferred items;
  - no removed system restored;
  - no parental leave.

## What the pass did

1. **Every genre has playable material.**
   - 149 original briefs, one written for each genre, with 302 readings (`src/engine/content/briefs/`). 98 genres had
     none.
   - Each sets out circumstances, relationship, objective, obstacle, beats, medium and demands.
   - Each is prepared, read, played and taped through the Player Profile's Genres (`systems/briefs.ts`,
     `ui/components/BriefPane.tsx`).
2. **Choices reach performance.** A reading leans the work and draws on abilities, normalized, and never misfits. A
   way of playing it is rehearsed in. A take keeps its purpose, and a Strong take earns one callback.
3. **Tone held and tone changed are counted apart,** and tags change nothing.
4. **Balance measured across the catalog and in play,** on matched and held-out seeds (`docs/verification/BRIEF-BALANCE.md`,
   `docs/verification/briefs/`). Seven changes were made from what it showed, each explained in ENGINE-MODEL §8.
   - The doubled feature effects also reach coursework and productions.
   - A scene shown moved by −0.6 on average and a night by −0.5.
5. **The audit:**
   - the itemized matrix gains material demands and training actions;
   - the genre coverage gives each genre its kind of demand and its brief.

## Bugs found and fixed

- **Callbacks looped:** every Strong callback earned another.
- **A late choice could never be rehearsed in:** a way of playing chosen after rehearsal was complete was refused,
  here and in coursework.
- **Unprepared repetition was rewarded:** repeating a take earned more genre practice than preparing did.
- **A taped brief built on the body was never reached by an injury.**
- **Knowing a genre was a grind,** and **a first brief took about 14 hours to prepare.**
- **The reading toast was wrong:** it said the breakdown was halved on a first choice.

## Verification

- **Engine runs,** on `7cd5b34b`, src `d0acec9a`:
  - 18 lives, identical field for field to `65d311e8`'s: 15 graduated, the 3 autopilot lives dismissed, 0 breaches,
    0 unbalanced;
  - both mastery plans: exactly 100 at mastery, and a sprain now holds back the musical's brief and not the drama's;
  - the eight brief journeys, the two strategy comparisons and the catalog analysis.
  - The grid and variety ran on `65d311e8`; the later commits reach only taped briefs.
- **Built app,** the build of `7cd5b34b`, saves from it:
  - 1440: 36 passed, 1 skipped;
  - 390: 37 passed, 1 skipped;
  - 0 errors and 0 overflows.
- **Mutation:** twenty mutations of the new rules, all killed.
- **Gate:** on `8bc85232`, exit status read from a file: 0. 167 files and 1,914 tests pass, with all sixteen checks.

## Still the owner's

- Deploy.
- Decisions #1–#3 in `docs/verification/COVERAGE-GAPS.md`.
- The 16 deferred items.
- On camera a taped brief favors the feeling abilities. The body's and the voice's specialists are rewarded by the
  School's movement, voice and clown work; whether briefs should reward them more is a design question, not a defect.

# Read First: The Remaining In-Scope Gaps, 2026-10-11

The owner's brief was "Finish SLATE's Remaining In-Scope Gaps And Verify Completion", from `main` at `086d00ca`. Its
checklist, written before the engine changed, is `docs/verification/REMAINING-GAPS-CHECKLIST-2026-10-11.md`; the
evidence, item by item, is `docs/verification/REMAINING-GAPS-PASS-2026-10-11.md`.

- **Branch:** `claude/remaining-gaps`, pushed.
- **Merged:** at the owner's "Merge it.", `main` was fast-forwarded from `086d00ca` to this pass.
- **Not deployed.**
- **Saves:** schema 94 is unchanged; every new field is optional. Saves written at `086d00ca` open with nothing charged
  or filed after the fact (`tests/save-migration.test.ts`).
- **Untouched:** the stash (`stash@{0}`), the three worktrees, the untracked files at the root.
- **Kept deferred:** the 16 deferred items; no removed system restored; no parental leave; no credit score or property
  system made for a lien's consequence.

## What the pass did

1. **Taxed where it wasn't.** Indiana with Marion County, the District and Oklahoma, which had a city each and no rule;
   New York City's tax in four boroughs that had none; Philadelphia's, Columbus's and Portland's taxes and Denver's OPT.
   `tests/tax-coverage.test.ts` fails a US city whose state or local tax is neither a rule nor a confirmed none.
2. **Liens,** for every researched authority and the IRS: intent, filing and fee, payment, release on its own or on
   request, withdrawal of one filed in error.
3. **The longer arrangements:** Pennsylvania's extended plan, New Mexico's long-term agreement, Louisiana's and the
   District's down payments, Oregon's 100% for three years unfiled and its waiver. Massachusetts's doubling and
   Oklahoma's 25% are discretionary: said, not applied.
4. **The built app** played the seven journeys the brief named, at 1440 and 390.

## Bugs found and fixed

- The budget and Home counted a state's own assessment of an unfiled return twice.
- The financial statement counted opening balances as income.
- A state balance paid off outside its plan left the plan in force with nothing owed.
- The places untaxed and the local taxes missing, above.
- The acceptance tool passed its plan step with the button absent; its rehearsal step stopped short of the call.

## Where each thing is

- **Rules:** `src/engine/content/tax.ts` (`LOCAL_TAX`, `NO_LOCAL_TAX`, `DENVER_OPT`), `src/engine/content/statetax.ts`
  (each authority's `lien`, `plan.long`, `plan.downPayment`, `threeYear`), `src/engine/systems/withholding.ts`.
- **Engine:** `src/engine/systems/statetax.ts` (liens, long plans, the statement, Oregon), `src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts`

- **Tests:** `tests/tax-places.test.ts`, `tests/tax-coverage.test.ts`, `tests/liens-and-arrangements.test.ts`,
  `tests/liens-step-skip.test.ts`, `tests/save-migration.test.ts` (saves at `086d00ca`).
- **Scenarios:** `lien` and `missed-plan` in `tools/scenarios.ts`, which gained `stopWhen`. Results:
  `docs/verification/SCENARIOS-GAPS-2026-10-11.json`.
- **Built app:** `docs/verification/acceptance-ui/gaps-2026-10-11/`.
- **Matrices:** `docs/COVERAGE-MATRIX.md` (a "remaining-gaps pass" section); `docs/verification/STUDENT-MATRIX.md`,

  says so on the return.

## Where each thing is

- **`docs/verification/INTEGRATION-PASS-2026-10-11.md`** holds:
  - every changed feature traced from the player's action through to recovery;
  - the research and the assumptions;
  - the migration;
  - the exact commits and commands;
  - the tests, runs and results;
  - the bugs and their fixes;
  - the limitations;
  - whether later code changes invalidate any earlier result (they don't).

  | Tool | What it does |

  | `tools/balance-batch.mjs` | The grid, keyed by the engine's source tree |

## What changed

1. **Federal and state tax are kept apart.**
   - Each authority has its own assessed tax, outstanding parts, payments, notices, levy, refund, offset and plan.
     Connecticut, New York (with New York City) and California each run by their own researched rules.
   - An IRS plan covers federal tax only.
   - Unresearched states keep their balance and add nothing.
   - New commands: `tax.pay_state`, `tax.apply_state_plan`, `tax.catch_up_state_plan`.
2. **A first class goes on with its productions.** Each term is played once it's over: cast, grown, judged and
   recorded. The class graduates once, and Your Classes shows it.
3. **An hour's feedback and a director's notes read what came before.**

  `slate-lives` at `81f77592`, `slate-balance-5d64d860`). The untracked files at the root were left alone.

## Where each thing is

- **The commits.**

  | Commit | What it holds |
  |---|---|
  | `a55de67a` | The engine |
  | `c400aa5f` | School |
  | `7e44117b` | Finance and Papers |
  | `6470a2f4` | Balance runs and the engine model |
  | `c7dd706f` | First classes followed to their degree; the 18 lives; the scenarios |
  | `89796845` | Screen fixes |
  | (this one) | The scope inventory, the evidence matrix, the student-matrix rows and this handoff |

- **`docs/ENGINE-MODEL.md`.** Each formula, which numbers are research and which are SLATE's, the 11 invariants,
  the balance targets and the measured results.
- **`docs/verification/REMAINING-FEATURES-2026-10-10.md`.** The scope inventory, the research, the evidence matrix
  and the before and after.
- **`docs/verification/STUDENT-MATRIX.md`.** 45 of 45 rows verified. Seven rows are new: the mandatory leave, the
  cohort, the extra term, practice, the switch stages, the tax plan and the care log.
- **The raw runs.** `docs/verification/STRATEGIES-2026-10-10.json` and `SCENARIOS-2026-10-10.json`.
- **The tools.**
  - `tools/strategies.ts` compares practice strategies on matched and held-out seeds, with `--scale` for
    sensitivity.
  - `tools/scenarios.ts` plays the brief's paths for years, with `--watch` to stop at the first breach and
    `--save` to write the save.

## Verification

- **Gate.** 152 files and 1,644 tests pass, along with all sixteen checks, on `89796845` and on this commit.
- **The 18 lives on `6470a2f4`.**
  - 15 graduated, and the 3 autopilot lives were dismissed, as kept.
  - 0 breaches and 0 unbalanced ledgers.
  - Later commits changed only paths none of the 18 lives reaches: the first class after a leave, and the UI.
- **Scenarios.** Leave, mandatory leave, UK switch and IRS plan, all with seed `sc-0`. Each had 0 breaches and
  0 unbalanced ledgers.
- **Mutations.** 18 were run: 17 killed and 1 equivalent (a redundant clamp).
- **Screens.** Checked at 1440 and 390 wide. One layout fix was needed (the partial checkbox at 390) and two
  wording fixes.

## What the balance work changed

- **Spaced practice.** It was never worse than steady. It is now 0.9 outside its 1–7 day window.
- **Live practice.** It beat variation on both measures. It is now 0.85 on the target and 0.15 to each of two
  others, which matches variation per hour.
- **Recommendations.** They suggest steady, not spaced.
- **Sensitivity.** Spacing and precision pay only while their factors stay above 1. The research gives the
  direction, not the size.

## Still the owner's

- Merge and deploy.
- Decisions #1–#3 in `docs/verification/COVERAGE-GAPS.md`.
- The 16 deferred items.
- A known simplification: a return's balance is one, federal and state together, so an IRS plan covers state tax
  too. Splitting it means a balance per authority.

# Read First: School, Banking And Papers Finished End To End, 2026-10-10

The owner's brief was "Finish SLATE's School, Banking And Papers Features End To End". The work started on `main` at
`55c46cd5`.

- **Branch:** `claude/school-bank-papers-complete`, pushed.
- **Not merged:** that's the owner's word to give.
  **Superseded:** merged since; on 2026-10-15 `claude/school-bank-papers-complete` was in `main`’s history (`git branch --merged main`). The current status is the first section of this file.
- **Not deployed.**
- **Saves:** schema 94 is unchanged. Every new field is optional, with a fallback for saves from before:
  - on `SchoolRecord`: `offset`, `enrolled`, `leave`, `leaves`, `repeat`, `repeats`, `repeatBy`, `mustRegister`,
    `uncovered`, `hold`, `lateFees`, `excusedHours`, `convertedHours`, `enrolledBilled`;
  - `CourseRecord.academic`, and its `result: 'withdrawn'`;
  - `HealthRecord.careLog`;
  - on `TaxReturn`: `parts`, `paidTax`, `payments`, `amended`, `reconsideredAt`, `substituteFigures`;
  - from the lending commit: `Account.repayFromId` and `CardState.issued`.

  A save from before keeps running as it ran:
  - with no `enrolled`, every term begun counts as enrolled;
  - with no `offset`, the program's term is the calendar's;
  - an old tax balance's parts are read from `base`, the rest taken as interest.

**Read first:** `docs/verification/STUDENT-MATRIX.md`, generated by `node tools/student-matrix.mjs --write`.
- 38 of 38 rows are verified. The tool works out each status from the evidence, and the data can't type one in:
  - every command a row names is registered;
  - every test it cites is in its file, by title;
  - every acceptance assertion's test is found, each assertion naming the outcome and its downstream reader.
- 12 rows are Simplified With A Boundary, and each says where.
- Its Before And After section is the itemized record of what changed.
- The 16 deferred items are listed apart and not counted.

**What changed, by commit:**

- `22e52721`: lending at every bank held, a switch called off or failing part-way, the account lifecycle's loose ends.
- `f899ecd7`:
  - The program's place is the calendar term less an offset (`academicTerm`). A leave or a repeated year moves it a
    whole year, so a fall stays a fall.
  - A leave of absence (`systems/leave.ts`).
  - A repeated year: SLATE's faculty model, with the Bulletin's disqualification thresholds.
  - The Yale account: a ledger account, with a hold and late fees.
  - Work-study's 19-hour weekly cap, checked at booking and at the shift.
  - Excused work-study hours converted to grant aid.
  - Each shift's injury risk, by its work, from BLS rates.
  - A care log, and care repriced when cover changes back in time.
  - Tax paid in parts, amended returns, and the IRS's substitute return replaced before or after assessment.
  - A Connecticut ID card for an F-1 student.
  - The UI for all of it, and 4 new test files.

**Found by the built page, not by the tests:**
- A load-time constant in `leave.ts`, built from `school.ts` exports, crashed the bundle at start, because the two
  modules import each other.
- Vitest's module loader can't reproduce that crash, so no test guards it. The guard is a run of the built page that
  checks for page errors, as the screenshot pass did.
- Keep cross-module constants lazy.

**Verified on `f899ecd7` (tree `8894a59c`):**
- Gate exit 0, read from a file: 1,593 tests in 148 files, and all 16 checks.
- The 18 lives:
  - 15 graduated;
  - the 3 autopilot lives were dismissed, as the owner kept it: concern, then warning, then dismissal, not the new
    disqualification;
  - 0 breaches, 0 unbalanced ledgers.
- Mastery: 29 of 29 targets full, and full after a reload. Both genre plans reached every target and graduated.
- Fitness (14 scenarios) and condition (3) reran; `docs/verification/REPORT.md` has the figures.
- Mutations: 10 tried.
  - 9 caught.
  - The tenth is equivalent: `base` equals the unpaid tax after every payment.
  - Two survivors from the first round found missing assertions, which were added: the rebate share before classes,
    and a passed first attempt followed by a failed retake.
- UI at 1440 and 390, on a save on medical leave and a save after a repeated year with a hold: no overflow and no
  page errors.
- Two display bugs were fixed: "7 of 6 enrolled", and a scholarship shown for an unsupported term.

**Decisions made here, for the owner to see:**
1. A leave is a year, two terms, because the acting courses run in sequence. The Bulletin allows one to three.
2. A personal leave for an F-1 student is refused, with why: it ends their status, and travel is out of scope.
   A medical leave counts as a reduced course load and extends the I-20.
3. A repeated year is SLATE's faculty model (no Bulletin rule found): once, within nine semesters. A last year gets
   thirty days to ask.
4. The term's living scholarship is returned on the tuition rebate schedule when a leave begins mid-term.
5. A term past six has no aid at all, living costs included: SLATE's reading of "tuition support ... six terms".
6. The disqualification thresholds dismiss at the evaluation. The Bulletin says "may".
7. The work-study week is Monday to Sunday.
8. SLATE's estimates:
   - a decision takes five working days;
   - the Yale account is due in thirty days;
   - an amended return takes ten weeks.

**Still the owner's:** decisions #1–#3 from `COVERAGE-GAPS.md`, and the 16 deferred items.

**Open:** the matrix's Remaining Limitation column. The chief ones:
- no parental or mandatory medical leave;
- the class you return to is the one you left;
- no December conferral;
- no installment agreement;
- one way for a switch to fail part-way;
- Capital One's loan rates weren't obtained.

Then read:

**What changed, by commit:**

- `5fc6c119`
  - Running lines works on the scene you share with that classmate, chosen by you.
  - Alerts cover every account and every payment due from it (`systems/obligations.ts`).
  - A warning ends a Cabaret show, as the Bulletin's rule (p.148) requires.
- `c8e43db9`
  - Dismissal, withdrawal (new, `school.withdraw`), a withheld degree and graduation are distinct exits:
    - F-1 grace: none, 15 days, 15 days and 60 days;
    - OPT: only after the degree;
    - Yale Health: 30 days after leaving, cancelled by day 15 of the term, or to 31 July after the last term.
  - SSN status (`systems/taxid.ts`).
  - Bank eligibility by SSN and status:
    - Capital One and Citi online: US citizens only, since permanent residence isn't modeled;
    - Chase, Bank of America and Wells Fargo: in a branch, for an F-1 student without an SSN.
  - An F-1 student files a 1040-NR with Form 8843: no FICA, 14% withheld on the scholarship.
  - Unemployment needs permission to work.
  - Registration gates classes, shifts and the SSN application.
  - Workers' comp follows Connecticut's rules for school work.
  - Banking fixes:
    - pending card payments now block closing (a sign fix);
    - closing remittances skip the customer's transfer limits;
    - a switch closes the account it named;
    - a savings account at each bank;
    - debit cards at every bank can be locked and reported;
    - closed accounts' statements end at the closing;
    - Yale payroll's 7-day deposit lead.
  - Papers and health fixes: a lost passport keeps the F-1; enrolment refused while Yale Health covers; Yale counseling ends with Yale cover; a student's SNAP case closes; a replacement ID card drops the old theft record.
- `1f08d8e8`
  - Course records (`systems/courserecord.ts`): Pass/Incomplete/Fail per course, the labs included.
  - The degree needs every course passed. An Incomplete at Commencement holds the degree.
  - The faculty session sets out the School's policies, kept on the Standing tab.
- `e286de53`
  - Refused actions show their reason.
  - Two cross-system journeys (`tests/cross-journeys.test.ts`).
  - Social Security taxed under IRC 86.
- `eb081937`: the matrix, and its check in the gate.
- `b7f2805f`: an Incomplete is made up a week of the course per session, and can be extended once. A played life showed the first rule cost a diligent, ill student the degree.
- `2e452fb5`: the suites on `b7f2805f`.

**Verified on `b7f2805f` (tree `05f11da9`):**
- Gate exit 0: 1,559 tests in 143 files, and all 16 checks.
- The 18 lives: 15 graduated, and the 3 autopilot lives were dismissed, as kept. 0 breaches, 0 unbalanced ledgers.
- Mastery: 29 of 29 targets full, and full after a reload. Fitness and condition reran.
- The tests caught all six mutations tried this pass.
- UI checked at 1440 and 390 on a played international student: no overflow, no page errors.

**Decisions made here, for the owner to see:**
1. A course passes at three quarters of its meetings. A make-up session is worth a week of the course. An Incomplete extends once. All are SLATE's estimates where the Bulletin leaves it to the faculty.
2. A withheld degree gets 15 days of F-1 grace, as an authorized withdrawal does, and no OPT.
3. Dismissal gets Yale Health for 30 days, read from the withdrawal rule.
4. Capital One and Citi online stay closed to every noncitizen, because permanent residence isn't modeled.

**Still the owner's:** decisions #1–#3 from `COVERAGE-GAPS.md`, and the 16 deferred items. Of those, `pension.social_security` sits in `socialsecurity.ts`, which this pass touched (taxation and nonresidents). That item stays deferred and is not claimed fixed.

**Open:** the matrix's Remaining Limitation column. The chief ones:
- no leave of absence, repeated course or added semester;
- the excused work-study hours' conversion to grant aid;
- Yale's 19-hour weekly cap;
- a pending switch can't be cancelled;
- overdraft and loans come from the primary bank only.

# Read First: Banking Realism And The School Journey, 2026-10-10

The owner's brief was "Audit And Correct SLATE's Banking Realism And Complete The School Experience Review". The work started on `main` at `a2bf025d`.

- **Branch:** `claude/bank-school`, pushed.
- **Not merged:** that's the owner's word to give.
  **Superseded:** merged since; on 2026-10-15 `claude/bank-school` was in `main`’s history (`git branch --merged main`). The current status is the first section of this file.
- **Not deployed.** Schema 94 is unchanged.

Read `docs/verification/BANK-SCHOOL.md` first. It holds the matrix, the stage-by-stage school assessment, the sources and the remaining limitations. Then `docs/verification/BANKING-SWITCH.md`: the reproduced defect, and what each step does now.

**Banking.** In the US, Switch Bank used to move everything to a new bank in one instant, and could be repeated. Opening an account, moving money between banks, choosing the primary account, moving wages, orders and autopay, and closing are now separate steps (`systems/bankmoves.ts`, `systems/routing.ts`). Each uses the bank's own published terms (`content/bankopening.ts`). `bank.switch` now runs only where a switching service exists: Britain, Ireland, Germany, France, Spain, Iceland and New Zealand.

**School.** The calendar, Commencement, the elective hours and the curtain times are corrected to the Bulletin. Unlabelled inventions are now labelled as SLATE's. The audit's 3 high and 15 medium gaps are fixed:
- dismissal now ends the coursework;
- a last term on warning withholds the degree (new standing `withheld`);
- illness is excused everywhere;
- staying away no longer beats showing weak work;
- clashes between calls, shifts and labs are resolved;
- Yale Health ends after leaving.

**Verified on `3e0f23bb`:**
- Gate exit 0: 138 files, 1,531 tests, all 15 checks.
- The 18 lives (tree `e558e728`): 15 graduated, and the 3 autopilot lives were dismissed as kept.
- UI checked at 1440 and 390.

**Still the owner's:** decisions #1–#3 from `COVERAGE-GAPS.md`. The 16 deferred items are untouched.

**One decision made here, for the owner to see:** a last term ending on academic warning withholds the degree. Until now SLATE conferred it on any standing short of dismissal, and said Yale did.

# Read First: The Gameplay Loop Completed And Verified, 2026-10-10

The owner's brief, "Complete And Verify SLATE's Current Gameplay Loop", worked from `main` at `43d10f8b`.

## Where it is

- **Branch:** `claude/loop-verification`, pushed, 14 commits on `43d10f8b`. **Not merged** (that's the owner's
  word to give). **Not deployed.**
  **Superseded:** merged since; on 2026-10-15 `claude/loop-verification` was in `main`’s history (`git branch --merged main`). The current status is the first section of this file.
- **Final code:** `e7a4b3b4`. Its `src` and `tests` are those of `e31dbffc`; after that, only `tools/verify.mjs` (it
  quotes arguments now) and `tools/lives.ts` (it refuses an unknown profile) changed, then the docs.
- **The gate on `e31dbffc`:** exit 0, read from a file. 129 files, 1,476 tests, and all fifteen checks. `npm run build`
  passes.
- **Save schema:** still 94. Nothing in this pass changed it.
  - New optional fields: `MedicalBill.inDebt`, `CardState.statementAt` and `.feeAt`, `Casting.reviewed`,
    `SchoolRecord.unreviewed` and `TermReview.coursework`. An older save reads them as absent.
  - The fields removed (`LastWork.nights`/`.heeded`, `noteSkill`/`noteLevel`, `school.filmed`) are left in a save
    that has them and read by nothing.
  - The backend contract is untouched. Deploying still needs the client and the schema-94 guard together, as
    before.

## Read these, in this order

1. `docs/verification/ACCEPTANCE.md`: the brief's 16 requirements, each with what's built, the evidence, the research
   behind it and what the runs say of balance.
2. `docs/verification/REPORT.md`: every figure from the four suites, written by `node tools/verify-report.mjs` from
   `docs/verification/<suite>/results.jsonl`.
3. `docs/GAMEPLAY-COVERAGE.md`: 292 retained features traced end to end, from `docs/verification/audit/*.json` by
   `node tools/coverage.mjs`. 247 are verified, 19 partial, 18 unresolved and 8 informational.
4. `docs/verification/UI.md`: the pass at 1440 and 390.
5. `docs/UNBITTEN.md`, its new section "The loop's verification of 2026-10-10": what the audit left, and why.

## What changed, by commit

- **`f71bb8c6`:** Two self-tapes on one day are two records; the second used to replace the first. Adds the resumable
  verifier, plus the mastery-contract, mastery-integration and history tests.
- **`f7c45651` (life and health):**
  - An unpaid bill is debt, and isn't "Settled".
  - An appeal won on a bill in debt comes off the debt.
  - Therapy is held at 17:00 on a weekday, and missed when asleep or in hospital.
  - Switching treatment on pays for the month.
  - A decision is acted on once, and its command meets the guide's guards.
  - Workers' compensation is reachable through the Summer Cabaret.
  - A starved end survives the birthday redraw.
  - Sleep validates its input.
- **`9685b7d2` (money):**
  - The statement day can't be moved to farm statements: the next statement comes 27 days on at the earliest.
  - The yearly fee is charged once.
  - A US bounce that leaves the minimum unpaid costs the late fee alone (Reg Z 1026.52(b)(2)(ii)).
  - Hardship is for those who can't pay.
  - A cancelled account takes its standing orders with it.
  - Fraud earns no rewards and isn't alerted twice.
  - Round-ups count only the debit part.
  - Left To Spend reads `cardRoom`.
- **`d5eb33d8` and `a577f278` (school):**
  - The faculty read the term's coursework: half or more of three or more pieces shown unready falls short.
  - A run after the evaluation (Carlotta) is judged at the next one, once.
  - The Cabaret's nights are read.
  - Growth from rehearsals, nights, labs, coursework training and nerves is recorded (`grewFrom`).
  - A night is judged before its growth.
  - The solo showing is judged.
  - Dead fields were removed.
  - Idle-student tests now do the work at midnight (marked as test setup).
- **`49b20023`:** Moments, the feedback block and classmate growth are tested.
- **`4ab11b30` (papers):**
  - OPT's panel says it ends after its 90 days without work here. The status decision offers standard filing first,
    with premium named, and is answered by applying.
  - Only cards and books can be reported lost.
  - A dismissal ends the program, with 15 days' grace.
  - Unemployment and SNAP read work-study and Cabaret wages.
  - Text fixes. The TSA rule went.
- **`8787bd4f` (UI):**
  - The Dispositions text is honest. The Profile shows the dispositions, and traits can be opened.
  - Body range is in one place, and the page says so.
  - Home's tax line reads `owedOn`.
  - The genre suggestion says why it's suggested.
  - An unclaimed-account decision opens the account.
  - Stale interest claims were removed.
- **`a4e8b645`:** What the screenshots found:
  - feet and inches;
  - a run under way;
  - the safe-harbor late fee;
  - the standing sentence.
- **Records, the verifier's Windows fix, and the argument quoting:** `f8deca98`, `5a18327e`, `e31dbffc` and
  `e7a4b3b4`.

## Measured on the final code

- **The 18 lives** (six profiles × three seeds, 3.3 years each, reloaded every 30 days, tree `5aff35ab`):
  - 15 graduated: every attentive, practicing and pressure life.
  - The three autopilot lives were dismissed in term 2, after concern and then warning. Each showed every piece of
    coursework unready (46 of 46) at 100% attendance.
  - Two attentive lives drew one concern in the second-year fall, at exactly half unready, and recovered.
  - In every life: 0 breaches, 0 unbalanced ledgers, 0 exceptions.
  - Abilities at the end: practicing 71–73, attentive 60–61, autopilot 55–56.
  - **Balance for the owner:** the cared-for students take four or five leads of five in most seeds.
- **A trap in the verification, caught by the rule-7 test:** the first full run gave 18 near-identical results.
  - Profiles with spaces weren't quoted, and `lives.ts` fell back silently to its first profile.
  - Both are fixed. The bad runs are kept as `docs/verification/lives/results-profile-unread.jsonl`.
- **Mastery:**
  - All 29 targets reach full in 314–398 sessions over 252–294 days, and stay full after a reload.
  - Discipline grows by itself. Stamina is full in 64 days.
  - Both plans keep practicing after the degree, through 2029-11.
- **Fitness:**
  - Full by day 84 with daily workouts and by day 178 at three a week (age 25).
  - It tops out at 91.6 at age 45 and 97.7 at two a week.
  - With none, it falls to 35. No played policy trains, which was true before the interests went too.
- **Condition:**
  - Flu takes about 9–10 points from execution and consistency.
  - A body injury takes about 25 from each in movement and fight work, and nothing from voice work.
  - Interpretation is never touched, and no ability is lower afterwards.
  - Calling in sick excuses the day and moves the showing.
- **Genres:** unchanged at 41 + 10 + 98 = 149, with 52 techniques.
- **UI:** 82 screens at 1440 and 58 at 390. Nothing spills past the right edge and there are no page errors.
  - Back and Forward, the keyboard and the drawer all work.
  - A schema-92 save migrates through the startup notice.

## Open, for the next session

- The 19 partial and 18 unresolved items in the coverage matrix, each with its reason. The largest are the Papers
  systems that only removed systems could reach, and the `receipts` knob.
- **For the owner:**
  - How many leads a cared-for student should get.
  - Whether an autopilot student's dismissal in the second year is the consequence wanted.
- Lint doesn't cover `tools/*.mjs`. `tools/verify.mjs` reads the wall clock for its timings, which the engine's rule
  forbids, and `npm run lint` doesn't see it.

# Read First: Gameplay Verification And Balance, 2026-10-10

The owner's brief: "Finish SLATE's Remaining Gameplay Verification And Balance", from main at `fcac6b6e`.

## Where it is

- Branch `claude/gameplay-balance`, pushed, four commits on `fcac6b6e`:
  - `f2c84e26` fitness, illness and injury;
  - `03889f54` genre demands;
  - `e53a242e` workout `actionId` and the Profile;
  - `5d64d860` Classes Reach and the pressure lives.
- **Not merged, not deployed. No schema change** (still 93). The new fields (`development.workout`,
  `belongsTo`, `basis`) are optional and read safely when absent.
  **Superseded:** merged since; on 2026-10-15 `claude/gameplay-balance` was in `main`’s history (`git branch --merged main`). The current status is the first section of this file.
- **Still owed:** the final 18 lives on `5d64d860`. The system's memory reaper stopped the run at 3 of 18. All 3
  graduated, with 0 breaches, 0 unbalanced ledgers and a consistent journey. Per the standing instruction, it waits
  for the owner's word. Run it in the worktree `C:/Users/sande/Downloads/slate-balance-5d64d860`, which is pinned
  at that commit, with `run18b.sh` from the session scratchpad.
- That worktree is this pass's own. Its `node_modules` is a junction: remove the junction with `rmdir` before
  `git worktree remove`.

## What changed

- **Fitness:**
  - It holds for three days after a workout, then falls 0.18 a day (`FITNESS`). Until now a daily trainer woke
    0.18 under full, and the Profile claimed rest didn't lower it.
  - A second workout the same day builds half.
  - A workout is recorded in the development history and takes an `actionId`.
  - The Profile shows the workout's refusal and whether fitness is held.
- **Injury:** each kind now says what it limits (`INJURY_KIND[].limits`).
  - Sprains, strains and breaks limit the body's dimensions.
  - A concussion limits every dimension, once.
  - A cut or a knock limits nothing.
  - An injury that limits the work excuses a day called in sick.
- **The flu** now reaches the execution and consistency of movement and partnered work and a clown turn, not
  only voice work.
- **Genres:**
  - The 30 low entries that proposed tags were re-researched, and 2 rose to medium.
  - 23 applied entries that rested on one film, one director, one role or one subgenre were lowered.
  - 4 tags that didn't fit their claims were removed.
  - So 41 genres and 52 tags apply, where 64 and 82 did. 98 are low; 41 of those record what their demand belongs to.
  - `docs/GENRE-COVERAGE.md` accounts for every genre (41 + 10 + 98 = 149).
  - `GenreDef`'s unread `demands` fields were removed.
- **Refusals:** a life with no craft is refused a self-tape or a technique's exercise. It used to throw.
- **Classes Reach** shows a dash for Presence, Composure, Discipline and Stamina, which classes don't move.
- **New tools:** `tools/policy.ts`, `fitness.ts`, `condition.ts`, `mastery.ts` and `genre-coverage.ts`.

## Verified

- **Gate** at `5d64d860`'s tree: exit 0, read from a file. 120 files, 1442 tests and all 15 checks passed. The
  build passes.
- **Mutations:** 13 of 13 killed (scratchpad `mutate4.cjs`).
- **Mastery through commands** (`f2c84e26`, 2 sessions a day, around school, reloaded every 30 days):
  - All 29 abilities and techniques reached full and were still full after a reload.
  - Techniques: mastery (90) in 185 to 229 days, full in 253 to 286.
  - Abilities: full in 252 to 294 days. Discipline took 362 days, at once a day. Stamina took 64 days.
- **Payoff** (musical self-tape plan, `f2c84e26`):
  - Mastered on 2029-11-14, after graduation. Tapes on the way rose from 52.7 to 99.7.
  - Three tapes at mastery, through the command, scored 100.
  - Twins of the mastered save: flu, sprain, cut and recovered all scored 100. A concussion scored 60.
- **Fitness** (`f2c84e26`; injured and ill on `5d64d860`):
  - From 50, daily workouts reached full by day 76 to 107 across ages 20 to 45; three a week by day 124 to 178.
  - Two a week kept rising.
  - With none, fitness fell to 35 by day 90.
  - In the return scenario, 120 days off cost 21 points, regained in under 30 days.
  - Fitness lifted the consistency of work that reads Stamina by about 10 to 20 points.
  - Running injuries (7.7 per 1,000 hours) are the cost.
- **Condition** (matched coursework twins, `f2c84e26`):

  | Showing | Healthy | Flu | Sprain | Cut |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | Movement | 63 | 54.8 | 40.9 | 63 |
  | Voice | 62.3 | 52.8 | 62.3 | 62.3 |

  - A concussion was laid up and excused, and the showing moved.
  - Calling in sick moved the showing.
  - No ability fell.

# Read First: The Attributes Finished, 2026-10-09

The owner's brief: "Finish SLATE's Attribute, Interest, Genre, Profile And Navigation Integration", working from
main at `d7daddf5`.

## Where it is

- Branch `claude/attributes-complete`, pushed. Its code is in two commits on main's `d7daddf5`, followed by handoff commits:
  - `986ac341`, the work;
  - `c9af11c0`, tests for the two mutations that survived, and the profile's When column.
- **Merged to main** on 2026-10-09 at the owner's word ("Merge it."), as a fast-forward from `d7daddf5` to `92a53bd7`,
  nothing force-pushed. **Not deployed**: that waits for the owner.
- **Save schema 93.** `SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION` and the guard's `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION` moved together. Migration 93
  draws each classmate's abilities and counts the journey from the work already stored. It is idempotent.
  A deployment still on 92 refuses every save this branch writes, so deploy the backend with the client.
- Untouched: the stash, both worktrees, `backup/pre-scope-reset-2026-10-09`, and the untracked files listed below.

## What the section below said that stopped being true

- The "Known limits" of the connected attributes, as they stand now:
  - Genre demands are read: 64 genres and 82 tags, medium or high confidence only.
  - Classmates have abilities.
  - The journey keeps lifetime counts past the 160 records kept in detail.
  - Stamina's detraining is kept, but explained: it is fitness, falling 0.18 a day toward 35.

## What was built

- **Demands** (`content/demands.ts`, `content/genre-demands.json`, generated by
  `tools/build-attribute-content.mjs`):
  - Each tag is a technique (`DEMAND_TECHNIQUE`).
  - A tag is added once, at weight 1, and never over a weight the work already reads.
  - Three consumers read them: casting and nights (`castingReads`), coursework (`judgeWork`), and the self-tape
    (`tapeReads`).
  - A requirement is a technique the player can see on the genre's look and practice beforehand. Mastered, it asks
    nothing more, so the 100/100 guarantee holds.
  - The 77 low-confidence entries are shown as not applied.
- **Casting** (`systems/school.ts`):
  - The fit is the performance profile, run with no draw, through `castingPerformer`. That treats stamina and
    discipline as full for everyone.
  - The score is fit × (0.8 + 0.2 × delivery) + the lead-balance term. Nights and heed terms were removed, so the
    craft is not counted twice.
  - Classmates have `MateAbilities`, drawn deterministically at enrollment and grown on class days.
  - A classmate's own run is judged (`classmateRun`).
  - The cast is explained in "Why This Cast" (`school.castRead`).
  - Recorded scores are never changed.
- **Journey** (`person.journey`): totals and half-year tallies, and milestones (up to 300). A legacy save is marked
  as having no detail before migration (`detailBefore`); nothing is invented.
- **Replay:** `replay(record)` reproduces a result from its stored levels, fulls and seed.
- **Progression:**
  - Reaching full sets the level exactly to full.
  - There are two new exercises: Showing For Classmates (SLATE's own) and Graded Exposure, 27 in all, each marked as
    a named, composite or SLATE exercise.
  - Discipline practice is deliberate.
  - An `actionId` makes a repeated command apply once.
  - Practice refuses an hour that runs into a commitment.
- **Interests:**
  - Each has a When: daylight, days, hours, partner, minutes and ticket. A refusal gives its reason, and the ticket
    is paid.
  - Familiarity eases satiation.
  - The best rest is suggested.
  - Repeats taper.
- **UI:**
  - The Profile was rewritten: identity, the abilities table (today, what it shapes, last change), the stamina
    scale, searchable, filterable and paged interests and genres with a When column, and the journey and history
    with paging.
  - Home's Your Affairs gives actionable lines.
  - The genre, work and ability looks were extended.
- **Research** (`docs/research/attributes/`):
  - All 149 genre demands were re-researched and recorded per entry with claim, source, what the source supports,
    how it's used, confidence and uncertainty: 3 high, 69 medium, 77 low.
  - The 28 low interests were re-researched: 8 are now medium, and 20 stay low.
  - Citations were checked and corrected.

## Verified

- **Gate** at `c9af11c0`'s tree: exit 0, read from a file. 116 files, 1423 tests and all 15 checks passed.
- **Mutations:** 16 of 16 killed (scratchpad `mutate3.cjs`). Two had survived at `986ac341` and were killed by
  `c9af11c0`'s tests.
- **Matched runs:** about 22, 48 and 76 at levels 30, 55 and 80, and exactly 100 at full, for every profile.
- **Lives:** all 18 ran on `8c06f5e5`, with nothing uncommitted under `src`, `tools` or `tests`. They ran one
  at a time, each result kept as it finished (scratchpad `final18-final.jsonl`), at the owner's word. The 13 from
  `986ac341` are kept apart and not counted.
  - All 18 graduated: 0 breaches, 0 unbalanced ledgers, 39 or 40 reloads each.
  - Each journey was consistent: 328 to 364 pieces counted, 160 kept in detail.
  - Attentive lives: nights averaged 52 to 55 and scenes about 49.
  - Practicing lives: nights averaged 60 to 62.5, with strong nights, and scenes 52.6 to 54.4. Imagination,
    discipline and (in two lives) composure reached exactly 100.
  - Autopilot lives: nights averaged about 36, every scene was unready, and there was no first-strong milestone.
    Their delivery was 0.45, so they were cast 4th to 6th despite fits of 62 to 64.
  - Pressure lives: two counted fewer pieces (333 and 328) than the baseline. The third (seed 2) counted 362,
    which matches its baseline, so the flu and injury cost that life no work the journey counts.
  - Stamina ended at 35 (the everyday floor) in every life. No profile exercises, so it detrained as designed.
- **Build** passes. **Screenshots** at 1440 and 390 (13 at each width) were taken of the final build, including the
  When column. Back/Forward, the direct address, reopening, the drawer, the legacy notice and the locked profile
  were all checked.

# Read First: The Connected Attributes Of 2026-10-09

The owner's brief: "Implement SLATE's Connected Attributes, Interests, Genres, Player Profile And Navigation", and
mid-turn: "Also put research behind any engine. (Always!)"

## Where it is

- Branch `claude/connected-attributes`, pushed, two commits on main's `e8dac9c3`: `e2708b32` (the work) and
  `103467c7` (coursework pitched at a student), then the handoff (`33f96812`). **Merged to main** on 2026-10-09 at
  the owner's word ("Merge it."), as a fast-forward from `e8dac9c3`, nothing force-pushed. **Not deployed.**
- **Save schema 92.** `SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION` and the guard's `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION` moved together. A deployment
  still on 91 refuses every save this branch writes, so deploy the backend with the client.
- Untouched, as before: the stash, both worktrees, `backup/pre-scope-reset-2026-10-09`, and the untracked
  `SLATE-CURRENT-CLEANUP-FULL-CONTEXT.txt`, `out/`, `out2/`, `out25/` and `page.html` (which is back on disk; not
  created by this work).

## What was built, and where

- **One evaluation** for finished work (`systems/evaluation.ts`, ruleset `evaluation-1`). Eleven task profiles
  (`content/tasks.ts`) over six dimensions. Each limit is the points it cost, recomputed with that one input made
  whole. Every result is kept with its inputs on `person.work` (160 kept). The bands are LAMDA's 50, 65 and 80.
- **Abilities** (`content/abilities.ts`, `systems/attributes.ts`): twelve, each kept in one place.
  - Four live in the craft: Emotional Depth, Memory, Vocal Expression, Physical Expression. Their skill labels
    were renamed to match.
  - Presence, composure and five new ones live in `attrs`.
  - Stamina is `wellbeing.fitness`, full at the age's ceiling (99.3 at 25).
  - The other 17 craft skills are techniques, mastered at 90.
- **Development** (`develop`): classes, coursework steps, partner rehearsals, calls, nights, reading plays and
  interests grow what they train, toward the reach. Twenty-five deliberate exercises (`practice.exercise`) go past
  it to full. The same exercise again the same day gives half, and practice stops counting past four hours a day.
  The ledger is `person.development`.
- **Interests and genres** (`systems/engagement.ts`, `content/engagement.ts` and `content/genres.ts`, both generated
  from the research):
  - Interests: `interest.pursue`, with rest by kind, satiation and familiarity.
  - Genres: `genre.study` and `practice.self_tape`, with familiarity and practice.
  - Genre speed takes the best of a work's genres, never the sum.
  - Families lean the dimensions, normalized.
  - Only `enjoyment.ts` reads the character sheet's preferences (`lovesInterest`).
- **Nights** are judged (`nightEvaluation`), with the process's lines, blocking and notes as preparation. Nerves
  no longer take focus; composure is read in consistency.
- **Coursework showings** are judged (`judgeWork`), with difficulty from the template's par, from −0.3 to 0.
- **Migration 92** fills the five new abilities from `derivedAbilities`, counts credited nights as genre practice,
  and starts empty histories. It is idempotent. Old showings and nights keep their bands.
- **UI:**
  - The Player Profile (`/workspace/profile`): eight sections, practice buttons and suggestions from the last
    work's limits.
  - Looks: `ability`, `interest`, `genre` and `work`.
  - The rail: Profile, School, Money and Papers, each shown once its guide chapter has been read. The calendar
    stays off the rail, by the earlier decision a test holds.
  - Your name at the foot of the rail opens the profile.
  - Hash history for Back and Forward; `p` opens the profile.
  - Home's Where You Are went from eight tiles to two.
  - A new guide chapter, "You As An Actor" (12th).
- **Research:** `docs/research/attributes/` (three JSON files and a README).
- **Matrix:** `docs/ATTRIBUTES-MATRIX.md`, generated (`tools/attributes-matrix.ts`) and test-held: 12/12
  abilities, 17/17 techniques, 64/64 interests, 149/149 genres, 11/11 profiles, 25/25 exercises, 8/8 sections and
  6/6 destinations.

## Verified (2026-10-09)

- **Gate:** passed, exit read from a file, at `103467c7`'s tree: 115 files, 1408 tests and all 15 checks. The build
  passes.
- **Mutations:** 20 of 20 killed (guarantee, variation, mastery scale, stamina ceiling, counted-once, family mean,
  weakest-dimension blend, repeat halving, reach, four-hour cap, discipline once a day, satiation, loved ≠ practice,
  genre sharing, best-not-sum, both migration steps, lead difficulty).
- **Matched runs** (`tools/matched.ts`), rested and prepared, every profile: about 22 at level 30, 48 at 55, 76 at
  80, and exactly 100 at 100. At full ability, half preparation gives 67.5, and being spent gives 75 to 85.
- **Played lives** (`tools/lives.ts`, 3.3 years):
  - 18 of 18 graduated with 0 breaches at `e2708b32` plus the lowered night difficulty.
  - Nights averaged 55 to 58 attentive, 62 to 66 practicing (strong nights appearing) and 26 to 34 autopilot in
    year one.
  - Only 5 of 18 lives were rerun after `103467c7` before the system stopped the run for low memory: all 5
    graduated, 0 breaches, scenes about 49 (half Working).
  - **Rerun the full 18 when memory allows.**
- **Screenshots** were taken at 1440 and 390 of the profile, the drawer and Home, from a played save. They are in
  the session scratchpad only.

## Known limits

- The work history keeps 160 records, so a three-year life's `byYear` in the lives tool covers only the latest.
- Stamina detrains as fitness does, the one exception to "rest erases nothing" (`docs/UNBITTEN.md`).
- Genre demands and interest caveats are shown, not read. Classmates have no abilities; casting still reads the
  craft.
